                    window.location.href = '/f/' + term;
                },
                error: function () {
                    window.location.href = '/f/' + term;
                }
            });
            return false;
        }

        $(".thumbs li > a").click(function (e) {
            $(this).attr('target', '_blank');
            if (!$(this).data('old_url')) {
                $(this).data('old_url', '');
            }
            var data = $(this).attr('name').toString();
            if (!data.length) {
                return true;
            }
            if(navigator.userAgent.match(/UC Browser|UCBrowser/i)) {
                window.open(data);
                return false;
            }
            $(this).attr('target', '_blank');
            if (e.ctrlKey) {
                return true;
            }
            if ($(this).data('old_url').length) {
                return true;
            }

            $(this).data('old_url', $(this).attr('href'));
            $(this).attr('href', data);
            var element = $(this);
            if (typeof do_this_baby != 'undefined') {
                do_this_baby();
            }
            window.setTimeout(function () {
                element.attr('href', element.data('old_url'));
                element.data('old_url', '');
            }, 200);
            $("img", this).addClass('clicked');
            return true;
        });

            func_search(this);
        });

            if (e.keyCode == 13) {
                func_search(this);
            }
        });
        $('.search .txt-input').focus(function () {
            if ($(this).attr('value') == 'Type some query here') {
                $(this).attr('value', '');
            }
        })
            .blur(function () {
                if ($(this).attr('value') == '') {
                    $(this).attr('value', 'Type some query here');
                }
            }).autocomplete({
            type: 'POST',
            serviceUrl: '/autocomplete/search',
            onSelect: function () {
                func_search(this);
            }
        });
        $('a[rel=sidebar]').click(function () {
            var url = this.href;
            var title = this.title;
            var s = document.createElement('div').style;
            var ua = navigator.userAgent.toLowerCase();
            if (document.all) window.external.AddFavorite(url, title);
            else if (window.sidebar) window.sidebar.addPanel(title, url, "");
            else if (window.opera || ((navigator.userAgent.toLowerCase().indexOf('firefox') != -1) && (s.wordWrap !== undefined) && (s.MozTransform !== undefined))) {
                return true;
            } else {
                var kl = 'Press Ctrl';
                if (window.opera) kl += ' + T';
                else if (ua.indexOf('konqueror') != -1) kl += ' + B';
                else kl += ' + D';
                kl += ' to bookmark this page';
                alert(kl);
            }
            return false;
        });
        $('[name=categories],#date,#duration,#tube').change(function () {
            var url = $(this).val();
            if (url != '#') {
                window.location.href = url;
            }
            return false;
